<entry name="!!HELP_DESC_03">You can get new creatures by purchasing them at the Treasure Cave or by defeating opposing creatures in battle. Sometimes, they will drop chests that you can keep.</entry>
<entry name="!!HELP_DESC_04">Each of your Creatures has an element that defines its strengths and weaknesses. Pink is Nicelands. Green is Swamp. Blue is Plains. Yellow is Corn. Tan is Sand!</entry>
<entry name="!!HELP_DESC_05">Nicelands creatures specialize in outlasting their foes through healing and beneficial buffs for their teammates.</entry>
<entry name="!!HELP_DESC_06">Sand Creatures are tough to kill! Their abilities are centered around taking hits and returning the damage back!</entry>
<entry name="!!HELP_DESC_07">Blue Plains Creature&apos;s Cards allow them to leech energy and devastate enemies with Magic Attacks.</entry>
<entry name="!!HELP_DESC_08">Corn teams are heavy hitters. They will crush your opponents with massive Drag Attacks.</entry>
<entry name="!!HELP_DESC_09">Swamp Creatures can apply status effects that cripple your opponent and deal damage over time.</entry>
<entry name="!!HELP_DESC_10">The Lab allows you to Power up and Enhance your creatures! Using the lab will increase your Creature&apos;s stats, Level, and Star Rating!</entry>
<entry name="!!HELP_DESC_11">If you are struggling in battle you can fuse Cake into your stronger fighters to make them even more powerful. Note that if you fuse a Cake of the same color as the creature you&apos;re powering up, it will grant 1.5 times the experience points. If you are low on Cake you can also fuse your extra Creatures into your target with the same results.</entry>
<entry name="!!HELP_DESC_12">Enhancing a Creature allows it to reach a new level of power! Find the correct ingredients and save up the coins and your Creature will gain a star level! Gaining a Star level increases the Creature&apos;s maximum level.</entry>
<entry name="!!HELP_DESC_14">Extra card slots allow the customization of a creature&apos;s cards. Experiment with different combinations to find what works best for your play style.</entry>
